-- [MEEN HUB] Anti-Debug / Anti-Tamper check (inert: measures only, never aborts)
do
  local __mh_t0 = os.clock()
  local __mh_sum = 0
  for __mh_i = 1, 50000 do __mh_sum = __mh_sum + (__mh_i % 7) end
  local __mh_dt = os.clock() - __mh_t0
end
